# Dhammasaṅgaṇīpāḷi ^0

Namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa ^1

<h1>Abhidhammapiṭake</h1> ^2

<h1>Dhammasaṅgaṇīpāḷi</h1> ^3

<h3>Mātikā</h3> ^4

<h2>1. Tikamātikā</h2> ^5

(Ka) kusalā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) akusalā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) abyākatā dhammā. ^6

(Ka) sukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) dukkhāya vedanāya sampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) adukkhamasukhāya vedanāya sampayuttā dhammā. ^7

(Ka) vipākā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) vipākadhammadhammā. ⤵ (Ga) nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammā. ^8

(Ka) upādiṇṇupādāniyā [upādinnupādāniyā (syā.)] dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anupādiṇṇupādāniyā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) anupādiṇṇaanupādāniyā [anupādinnānupādāniyā (syā.)] dhammā. ^9

(Ka) saṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) asaṃkiliṭṭhasaṃkilesikā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) asaṃkiliṭṭhaasaṃkilesikā [asaṃkiliṭṭhāsaṃkilesikā (syā.)] dhammā. ^10

(Ka) savitakkasavicārā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) avitakkavicāramattā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) avitakkaavicārā [avitakkāvicārā (syā.)] dhammā. ^11

(Ka) pītisahagatā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) sukhasahagatā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) upekkhāsahagatā dhammā. ^12

(Ka) dassanena pahātabbā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā. ^13

(Ka) dassanena pahātabbahetukā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) neva dassanena na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā. ^14

(Ka) ācayagāmino dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) apacayagāmino dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) nevācayagāmināpacayagāmino [nevācayagāmino nāpacayagāmino (syā.)] dhammā. ^15

(Ka) sekkhā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) asekkhā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) nevasekkhanāsekkhā [nevasekkhānāsekkhā (sī. syā.)] dhammā. ^16

(Ka) parittā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) mahaggatā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) appamāṇā dhammā. ^17

(Ka) parittārammaṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) mahaggatārammaṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) appamāṇārammaṇā dhammā. ^18

(Ka) hīnā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) majjhimā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) paṇītā dhammā. ^19

(Ka) micchattaniyatā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) sammattaniyatā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) aniyatā dhammā. ^20

(Ka) maggārammaṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) maggahetukā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) maggādhipatino dhammā. ^21

(Ka) uppannā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anuppannā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) uppādino dhammā. ^22

(Ka) atītā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anāgatā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) paccuppannā dhammā. ^23

(Ka) atītārammaṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anāgatārammaṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) paccuppannārammaṇā dhammā. ^24

(Ka) ajjhattā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) bahiddhā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) ajjhattabahiddhā dhammā. ^25

(Ka) ajjhattārammaṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) bahiddhārammaṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) ajjhattabahiddhārammaṇā dhammā. ^26

(Ka) sanidassanasappaṭighā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anidassanasappaṭighā dhammā. ⤵ (Ga) anidassanaappaṭighā [anidassanāppaṭighā (syā.)] dhammā. ^27

Tikamātikā. ^28

<h1>2. Dukamātikā</h1> ^29

<h2>Hetugocchakaṃ</h2> ^30

(Ka) hetū dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na hetū dhammā. ^31

(Ka) sahetukā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) ahetukā dhammā. ^32

(Ka) hetusampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) hetuvippayuttā dhammā. ^33

(Ka) hetū ceva dhammā sahetukā ca. ⤵ (Kha) sahetukā ceva dhammā na ca hetū. ^34

(Ka) hetū ceva dhammā hetusampayuttā ca. ⤵ (Kha) hetusampayuttā ceva dhammā na ca hetū. ^35

(Ka) na hetū kho pana dhammā sahetukāpi. ⤵ (Kha) ahetukāpi. ^36

Hetugocchakaṃ. ^37

<h2>Cūḷantaradukaṃ</h2> ^38

(Ka) sappaccayā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) appaccayā dhammā. ^39

(Ka) saṅkhatā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) asaṅkhatā dhammā. ^40

(Ka) sanidassanā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anidassanā dhammā. ^41

(Ka) sappaṭighā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) appaṭighā dhammā. ^42

(Ka) rūpino dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) arūpino dhammā. ^43

(Ka) lokiyā dhammā . ⤵ (Kha) lokuttarā dhammā. ^44

(Ka) kenaci viññeyyā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) kenaci na viññeyyā dhammā. ^45

Cūḷantaradukaṃ. ^46

<h2>Āsavagocchakaṃ</h2> ^47

(Ka) āsavā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no āsavā dhammā. ^48

(Ka) sāsavā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anāsavā dhammā. ^49

(Ka) āsavasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) āsavavippayuttā dhammā. ^50

(Ka) āsavā ceva dhammā sāsavā ca. ⤵ (Kha) sāsavā ceva dhammā no ca āsavā. ^51

(Ka) āsavā ceva dhammā āsavasampayuttā ca. ⤵ (Kha) āsavasampayuttā ceva dhammā no ca āsavā. ^52

(Ka) āsavavippayuttā kho pana dhammā sāsavāpi. ⤵ (Kha) anāsavāpi. ^53

Āsavagocchakaṃ. ^54

<h2>Saṃyojanagocchakaṃ</h2> ^55

(Ka) saṃyojanā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no saṃyojanā dhammā. ^56

(Ka) saṃyojaniyā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) asaṃyojaniyā dhammā. ^57

(Ka) saṃyojanasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) saṃyojanavippayuttā dhammā. ^58

(Ka) saṃyojanā ceva dhammā saṃyojaniyā ca. ⤵ (Kha) saṃyojaniyā ceva dhammā no ca saṃyojanā. ^59

(Ka) saṃyojanā ceva dhammā saṃyojanasampayuttā ca. ⤵ (Kha) saṃyojanasampayuttā ceva dhammā no ca saṃyojanā. ^60

(Ka) saṃyojanavippayuttā kho pana dhammā saṃyojaniyāpi. ⤵ (Kha) asaṃyojaniyāpi. ^61

Saṃyojanagocchakaṃ. ^62

<h2>Ganthagocchakaṃ</h2> ^63

(Ka) ganthā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no ganthā dhammā. ^64

(Ka) ganthaniyā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) aganthaniyā dhammā. ^65

(Ka) ganthasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) ganthavippayuttā dhammā. ^66

(Ka) ganthā ceva dhammā ganthaniyā ca. ⤵ (Kha) ganthaniyā ceva dhammā no ca ganthā. ^67

(Ka) ganthā ceva dhammā ganthasampayuttā ca. ⤵ (Kha) ganthasampayuttā ceva dhammā no ca ganthā. ^68

(Ka) ganthavippayuttā kho pana dhammā ganthaniyāpi. ⤵ (Kha) aganthaniyāpi. ^69

Ganthagocchakaṃ. ^70

<h2>Oghagocchakaṃ</h2> ^71

(Ka) oghā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no oghā dhammā. ^72

(Ka) oghaniyā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anoghaniyā dhammā. ^73

(Ka) oghasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) oghavippayuttā dhammā. ^74

(Ka) oghā ceva dhammā oghaniyā ca. ⤵ (Kha) oghaniyā ceva dhammā no ca oghā. ^75

(Ka) oghā ceva dhammā oghasampayuttā ca. ⤵ (Kha) oghasampayuttā ceva dhammā no ca oghā. ^76

(Ka) oghavippayuttā kho pana dhammā oghaniyāpi. ⤵ (Kha) anoghaniyāpi. ^77

Oghagocchakaṃ. ^78

<h2>Yogagocchakaṃ</h2> ^79

(Ka) yogā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no yogā dhammā. ^80

(Ka) yoganiyā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) ayoganiyā dhammā. ^81

(Ka) yogasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) yogavippayuttā dhammā. ^82

(Ka) yogā ceva dhammā yoganiyā ca. ⤵ (Kha) yoganiyā ceva dhammā no ca yogā. ^83

(Ka) yogā ceva dhammā yogasampayuttā ca. ⤵ (Kha) yogasampayuttā ceva dhammā no ca yogā. ^84

(Ka) yogavippayuttā kho pana dhammā yoganiyāpi. ⤵ (Kha) ayoganiyāpi. ^85

Yogagocchakaṃ. ^86

<h2>Nīvaraṇagocchakaṃ</h2> ^87

(Ka) nīvaraṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no nīvaraṇā dhammā. ^88

(Ka) nīvaraṇiyā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anīvaraṇiyā dhammā. ^89

(Ka) nīvaraṇasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) nīvaraṇavippayuttā dhammā. ^90

(Ka) nīvaraṇā ceva dhammā nīvaraṇiyā ca. ⤵ (Kha) nīvaraṇiyā ceva dhammā no ca nīvaraṇā. ^91

(Ka) nīvaraṇā ceva dhammā nīvaraṇasampayuttā ca. ⤵ (Kha) nīvaraṇasampayuttā ceva dhammā no ca nīvaraṇā. ^92

(Ka) nīvaraṇavippayuttā kho pana dhammā nīvaraṇiyāpi. ⤵ (Kha) anīvaraṇiyāpi . ^93

Nīvaraṇagocchakaṃ. ^94

<h2>Parāmāsagocchakaṃ</h2> ^95

(Ka) parāmāsā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no parāmāsā dhammā. ^96

(Ka) parāmaṭṭhā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) aparāmaṭṭhā dhammā. ^97

(Ka) parāmāsasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) parāmāsavippayuttā dhammā. ^98

(Ka) parāmāsā ceva dhammā parāmaṭṭhā ca. ⤵ (Kha) parāmaṭṭhā ceva dhammā no ca parāmāsā. ^99

(Ka) parāmāsavippayuttā kho pana dhammā parāmaṭṭhāpi. ⤵ (Kha) aparāmaṭṭhāpi. ^100

Parāmāsagocchakaṃ. ^101

<h2>Mahantaradukaṃ</h2> ^102

(Ka) sārammaṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anārammaṇā dhammā. ^103

(Ka) cittā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no cittā dhammā. ^104

(Ka) cetasikā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) acetasikā dhammā. ^105

(Ka) cittasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) cittavippayuttā dhammā. ^106

(Ka) cittasaṃsaṭṭhā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) cittavisaṃsaṭṭhā dhammā. ^107

(Ka) cittasamuṭṭhānā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no cittasamuṭṭhānā dhammā. ^108

(Ka) cittasahabhuno dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no cittasahabhuno dhammā. ^109

(Ka) cittānuparivattino dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no cittānuparivattino dhammā. ^110

(Ka) cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānā dhammā. ^111

(Ka) cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānasahabhuno dhammā. ^112

(Ka) cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no cittasaṃsaṭṭhasamuṭṭhānānuparivattino dhammā. ^113

(Ka) ajjhattikā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) bāhirā dhammā. ^114

(Ka) upādā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no upādā dhammā. ^115

(Ka) upādiṇṇā [upādinnā (sī. syā.)] dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anupādiṇṇā dhammā. ^116

Mahantaradukaṃ. ^117

<h2>Upādānagocchakaṃ</h2> ^118

(Ka) upādānā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no upādānā dhammā. ^119

(Ka) upādāniyā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anupādāniyā dhammā. ^120

(Ka) upādānasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) upādānavippayuttā dhammā. ^121

(Ka) upādānā ceva dhammā upādāniyā ca. ⤵ (Kha) upādāniyā ceva dhammā no ca upādānā. ^122

(Ka) upādānā ceva dhammā upādānasampayuttā ca. ⤵ (Kha) upādānasampayuttā ceva dhammā no ca upādānā. ^123

(Ka) upādānavippayuttā kho pana dhammā upādāniyāpi. ⤵ (Kha) anupādāniyāpi. ^124

Upādānagocchakaṃ. ^125

<h2>Kilesagocchakaṃ</h2> ^126

(Ka) kilesā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) no kilesā dhammā. ^127

(Ka) saṃkilesikā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) asaṃkilesikā dhammā. ^128

(Ka) saṃkiliṭṭhā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) asaṃkiliṭṭhā dhammā. ^129

(Ka) kilesasampayuttā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) kilesavippayuttā dhammā. ^130

(Ka) kilesā ceva dhammā saṃkilesikā ca. ⤵ (Kha) saṃkilesikā ceva dhammā no ca kilesā. ^131

(Ka) kilesā ceva dhammā saṃkiliṭṭhā ca. ⤵ (Kha) saṃkiliṭṭhā ceva dhammā no ca kilesā. ^132

(Ka) kilesā ceva dhammā kilesasampayuttā ca. ⤵ (Kha) kilesasampayuttā ceva dhammā no ca kilesā. ^133

(Ka) kilesavippayuttā kho pana dhammā saṃkilesikāpi. ⤵ (Kha) asaṃkilesikāpi. ^134

Kilesagocchakaṃ. ^135

<h2>Piṭṭhidukaṃ</h2> ^136

(Ka) dassanena pahātabbā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na dassanena pahātabbā dhammā. ^137

(Ka) bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na bhāvanāya pahātabbā dhammā. ^138

(Ka) dassanena pahātabbahetukā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na dassanena pahātabbahetukā dhammā. ^139

(Ka) bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na bhāvanāya pahātabbahetukā dhammā. ^140

(Ka) savitakkā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) avitakkā dhammā. ^141

(Ka) savicārā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) avicārā dhammā. ^142

(Ka) sappītikā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) appītikā dhammā. ^143

(Ka) pītisahagatā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na pītisahagatā dhammā. ^144

(Ka) sukhasahagatā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na sukhasahagatā dhammā. ^145

(Ka) upekkhāsahagatā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na upekkhāsahagatā dhammā. ^146

(Ka) kāmāvacarā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na kāmāvacarā dhammā. ^147

(Ka) rūpāvacarā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na rūpāvacarā dhammā. ^148

(Ka) arūpāvacarā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) na arūpāvacarā dhammā. ^149

(Ka) pariyāpannā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) apariyāpannā dhammā. ^150

(Ka) niyyānikā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) aniyyānikā dhammā. ^151

(Ka) niyatā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) aniyatā dhammā. ^152

(Ka) sauttarā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) anuttarā dhammā. ^153

(Ka) saraṇā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) araṇā dhammā. ^154

Piṭṭhidukaṃ. ^155

Abhidhammadukamātikā. ^156

<h2>Suttantikadukamātikā</h2> ^157

(Ka) vijjābhāgino dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) avijjābhāgino dhammā. ^158

(Ka) vijjūpamā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) vajirūpamā dhammā. ^159

(Ka) bālā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) paṇḍitā dhammā. ^160

(Ka) kaṇhā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) sukkā dhammā. ^161

(Ka) tapanīyā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) atapanīyā dhammā. ^162

(Ka) adhivacanā dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) adhivacanapathā dhammā. ^163

(Ka) nirutti dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) niruttipathā dhammā. ^164

(Ka) paññatti dhammā. ⤵ (Kha) paññattipathā dhammā. ^165

(Ka) nāmañca. ⤵ (Kha) rūpañca. ^166

(Ka) avijjā ca. ⤵ (Kha) bhavataṇhā ca. ^167

(Ka) bhavadiṭṭhi ca. ⤵ (Kha) vibhavadiṭṭhi ca. ^168

(Ka) sassatadiṭṭhi ca. ⤵ (Kha) ucchedadiṭṭhi ca. ^169

(Ka) antavā diṭṭhi ca. ⤵ (Kha) anantavā diṭṭhi ca. ^170

(Ka) pubbantānudiṭṭhi ca. ⤵ (Kha) aparantānudiṭṭhi ca. ^171

(Ka) ahirikañca. ⤵ (Kha) anottappañca. ^172

(Ka) hirī ca. ⤵ (Kha) ottappañca. ^173

(Ka) dovacassatā ca. ⤵ (Kha) pāpamittatā ca. ^174

(Ka) sovacassatā ca. ⤵ (Kha) kalyāṇamittatā ca. ^175

(Ka) āpattikusalatā ca. ⤵ (Kha) āpattivuṭṭhānakusalatā ca. ^176

(Ka) samāpattikusalatā ca. ⤵ (Kha) samāpattivuṭṭhānakusalatā ca. ^177

(Ka) dhātukusalatā ca. ⤵ (Kha) manasikārakusalatā ca. ^178

(Ka) āyatanakusalatā ca. ⤵ (Kha) paṭiccasamuppādakusalatā ca. ^179

(Ka) ṭhānakusalatā ca. ⤵ (Kha) aṭṭhānakusalatā ca. ^180

(Ka) ajjavo ca. ⤵ (Kha) maddavo ca. ^181

(Ka) khanti ca. ⤵ (Kha) soraccañca. ^182

(Ka) sākhalyañca. ⤵ (Kha) paṭisanthāro ca [paṭisandhāro ca (ka.)]. ^183

(Ka) indriyesu aguttadvāratā ca. ⤵ (Kha) bhojane amattaññutā ca. ^184

(Ka) indriyesu guttadvāratā ca. ⤵ (Kha) bhojane mattaññutā ca. ^185

(Ka) muṭṭhasaccañca. ⤵ (Kha) asampajaññañca. ^186

(Ka) sati ca. ⤵ (Kha) sampajaññañca. ^187

(Ka) paṭisaṅkhānabalañca. ⤵ (Kha) bhāvanābalañca. ^188

(Ka) samatho ca. ⤵ (Kha) vipassanā ca. ^189

(Ka) samathanimittañca . ⤵ (Kha) paggāhanimittañca. ^190

(Ka) paggāho ca. ⤵ (Kha) avikkhepo ca. ^191

(Ka) sīlavipatti ca. ⤵ (Kha) diṭṭhivipatti ca. ^192

(Ka) sīlasampadā ca. ⤵ (Kha) diṭṭhisampadā ca. ^193

(Ka) sīlavisuddhi ca. ⤵ (Kha) diṭṭhivisuddhi ca. ^194

(Ka) diṭṭhivisuddhi kho pana. ⤵ (Kha) yathādiṭṭhissa ca padhānaṃ. ^195

(Ka) saṃvego ca saṃvejaniyesu ṭhānesu. ⤵ (Kha) saṃviggassa ca yoniso padhānaṃ. ^196

(Ka) asantuṭṭhitā ca kusalesu dhammesu. ⤵ (Kha) appaṭivānitā ca padhānasmiṃ. ^197

(Ka) vijjā ca. ⤵ (Kha) vimutti ca. ^198

(Ka) khaye ñāṇaṃ. ⤵ (Kha) anuppāde ñāṇanti. ^199

Suttantikadukamātikā [suttantamātikā (syā.)]. ^200

Mātikā niṭṭhitā. ^201

<h3>1. Cittuppādakaṇḍaṃ</h3> ^202

<h1>Kāmāvacarakusalaṃ</h1> ^203

<h2>Padabhājanī</h2> ^204

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, pīti hoti, sukhaṃ hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ [viriyindriyaṃ (sī. syā.)] hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, paññindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, somanassindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, sammāsaṅkappo hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti, sammāsati hoti, sammāsamādhi hoti, saddhābalaṃ hoti, vīriyabalaṃ [viriyabalaṃ (sī. syā.)] hoti, satibalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, paññābalaṃ hoti, hiribalaṃ hoti, ottappabalaṃ hoti, alobho hoti, adoso hoti, amoho hoti, anabhijjhā hoti, abyāpādo hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, hirī hoti, ottappaṃ hoti, kāyapassaddhi [kāyappassaddhi (syā.)] hoti, cittapassaddhi [cittappassaddhi (syā.)] hoti, kāyalahutā hoti, cittalahutā hoti, kāyamudutā hoti, cittamudutā hoti, kāyakammaññatā hoti, cittakammaññatā hoti, kāyapāguññatā hoti, cittapāguññatā hoti, kāyujukatā [kāyujjukatā (sī. ka.)] hoti, cittujukatā [cittujjukatā (sī. ka.)] hoti, sati hoti, sampajaññaṃ hoti, samatho hoti, vipassanā hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^205

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti. ^206

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti. ^207

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti. ^208

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajā cetanā sañcetanā cetayitattaṃ [sañcetayitattaṃ (syā.)] – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti. ^209

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti. ^210

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti. ^211

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cāro vicāro anuvicāro upavicāro cittassa anusandhānatā anupekkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti. ^212

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye pīti pāmojjaṃ āmodanā pamodanā hāso pahāso vitti odagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti. ^213

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti. ^214

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti. ^215

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti. ^216

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho [viriyārambho (sī. syā.)] nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī [ussoḷhi (sī.)] thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti. ^217

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti. ^218

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti. ^219

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti. ^220

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti. ^221

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti. ^222

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tesaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā [irīyanā (sī.)] vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti. ^223

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti. ^224

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti. ^225

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti. ^226

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti. ^227

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti. ^228

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti. ^229

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti. ^230

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti. ^231

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti. ^232

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti. ^233

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirīyati hiriyitabbena hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti. ^234

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappati ottappitabbena ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti. ^235

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti. ^236

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ [adūsanā adūsitattaṃ (syā.)] abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti? ^237

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi amoho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti. ^238

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye anabhijjhā hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye anabhijjhā hoti. ^239

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye abyāpādo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye abyāpādo hoti. ^240

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti. ^241

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye hirī hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirīyati hiriyitabbena hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirī hoti. ^242

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappati ottappitabbena ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappaṃ hoti. ^243

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyapassaddhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭipassaddhi [paṭippassaddhi (sī. syā.)] passambhanā paṭipassambhanā [paṭippassambhanā (sī. syā.)] paṭipassambhitattaṃ [paṭippassambhitattaṃ (sī. syā.)] – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyapassaddhi hoti. ^244

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittapassaddhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa passaddhi paṭipassaddhi passambhanā paṭipassambhanā paṭipassambhitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittapassaddhi hoti. ^245

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyalahutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyalahutā hoti. ^246

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittalahutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa lahutā lahupariṇāmatā adandhanatā avitthanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittalahutā hoti. ^247

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyamudutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyamudutā hoti. ^248

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittamudutā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa mudutā maddavatā akakkhaḷatā akathinatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittamudutā hoti. ^249

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyakammaññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa kammaññatā kammaññattaṃ kammaññabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyakammaññatā hoti. ^250

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittakammaññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa kammaññatā kammaññattaṃ kammaññabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittakammaññatā hoti. ^251

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyapāguññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyapāguññatā hoti. ^252

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittapāguññatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa paguṇatā paguṇattaṃ paguṇabhāvo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittapāguññatā hoti. ^253

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye kāyujukatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandhassa saññākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye kāyujukatā hoti. ^254

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittujukatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandhassa ujutā ujukatā ajimhatā avaṅkatā akuṭilatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittujukatā hoti. ^255

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sati hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sati hoti. ^256

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sampajaññaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sampajaññaṃ hoti. ^257

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye samatho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samatho hoti. ^258

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vipassanā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vipassanā hoti. ^259

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye paggāho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paggāho hoti. ^260

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye avikkhepo hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye avikkhepo hoti. ⤵ Ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^261

Padabhājanīyaṃ. ^262

Paṭhamabhāṇavāro. ^263

<h2>Koṭṭhāsavāro</h2> ^264

Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, aṭṭhindriyāni honti, pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti, satta balāni honti, tayo hetū honti, eko phasso hoti, ekā vedanā hoti, ekā saññā hoti, ekā cetanā hoti, ekaṃ cittaṃ hoti, eko vedanākkhandho hoti, eko saññākkhandho hoti, eko saṅkhārakkhandho hoti, eko viññāṇakkhandho hoti, ekaṃ manāyatanaṃ hoti, ekaṃ manindriyaṃ hoti, ekā manoviññāṇadhātu hoti, ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^265

Katame tasmiṃ samaye cattāro khandhā honti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho. ^266

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandho hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandho hoti. ^267

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saññākkhandho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññākkhandho hoti. ^268

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro pīti cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ paññābalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso amoho anabhijjhā abyāpādo sammādiṭṭhi hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajaññaṃ samatho vipassanā paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā [thapetvā (ka.)] vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti. ^269

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandho hoti ? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandho hoti. ^270

Ime tasmiṃ samaye cattāro khandhā honti. ^271

Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye dvāyatanāni honti? Manāyatanaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ. ^272

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manāyatanaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manāyatanaṃ hoti. ^273

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammāyatanaṃ hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammāyatanaṃ hoti. ^274

Imāni tasmiṃ samaye dvāyatanāni honti. ^275

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye dve dhātuyo honti? Manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu. ^276

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye manoviññāṇadhātu hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manoviññāṇadhātu hoti. ^277

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye dhammadhātu hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammadhātu hoti. ^278

Imā tasmiṃ samaye dve dhātuyo honti. ^279

Katame tasmiṃ samaye tayo āhārā honti? Phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro , viññāṇāhāro. ^280

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phassāhāro hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phassāhāro hoti. ^281

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye manosañcetanāhāro hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā sañcetanā cetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manosañcetanāhāro hoti. ^282

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇāhāro hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇāhāro hoti. ^283

Ime tasmiṃ samaye tayo āhārā honti. ^284

Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye aṭṭhindriyāni honti? Saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ. ^285

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhindriyaṃ hoti. ^286

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyindriyaṃ hoti. ^287

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satindriyaṃ hoti. ^288

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhindriyaṃ hoti. ^289

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññindriyaṃ hoti. ^290

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti. ^291

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye somanassindriyaṃ hoti. ^292

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti? Yo tesaṃ arūpīnaṃ dhammānaṃ āyu ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā iriyanā vattanā pālanā jīvitaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jīvitindriyaṃ hoti . ^293

Imāni tasmiṃ samaye aṭṭhindriyāni honti. ^294

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti? Vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā. ^295

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vitakko hoti. ^296

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cāro vicāro anuvicāro upavicāro cittassa anusandhānatā anupekkhanatā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vicāro hoti. ^297

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye pīti pāmojjaṃ āmodanā pamodanā hāso pahāso vitti odagyaṃ attamanatā cittassa – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pīti hoti. ^298

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sukhaṃ hoti. ^299

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittassekaggatā hoti. ^300

Idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti. ^301

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti? Sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi. ^302

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammādiṭṭhi hoti. ^303

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye takko vitakko saṅkappo appanā byappanā cetaso abhiniropanā sammāsaṅkappo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsaṅkappo hoti. ^304

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāvāyāmo hoti. ^305

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsati hoti. ^306

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye sammāsamādhi hoti. ^307

Ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti. ^308

Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye satta balāni honti? Saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, paññābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, ottappabalaṃ. ^309

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saddhā saddahanā okappanā abhippasādo saddhā saddhindriyaṃ saddhābalaṃ – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saddhābalaṃ hoti. ^310

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye cetasiko vīriyārambho nikkamo parakkamo uyyāmo vāyāmo ussāho ussoḷhī thāmo dhiti asithilaparakkamatā anikkhittachandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāho vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmo – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vīriyabalaṃ hoti. ^311

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye sati anussati paṭissati sati saraṇatā dhāraṇatā apilāpanatā asammussanatā sati satindriyaṃ satibalaṃ sammāsati – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye satibalaṃ hoti. ^312

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhāro avikkhepo avisāhaṭamānasatā samatho samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye samādhibalaṃ hoti. ^313

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā vicayo pavicayo dhammavicayo sallakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā paccupalakkhaṇā paṇḍiccaṃ kosallaṃ nepuññaṃ vebhabyā cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī medhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajaññaṃ patodo paññā paññindriyaṃ paññābalaṃ paññāsatthaṃ paññāpāsādo paññāāloko paññāobhāso paññāpajjoto paññāratanaṃ amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye paññābalaṃ hoti. ^314

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hirīyati hiriyitabbena hirīyati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye hiribalaṃ hoti. ^315

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappati ottappitabbena ottappati pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ottappabalaṃ hoti. ^316

Imāni tasmiṃ samaye satta balāni honti. ^317

Katame tasmiṃ samaye tayo hetū honti? Alobho, adoso, amoho. ^318

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye alobho alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ asārāgo asārajjanā asārajjitattaṃ anabhijjhā alobho kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye alobho hoti. ^319

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye adoso adussanā adussitattaṃ abyāpādo abyāpajjo adoso kusalamūlaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye adoso hoti. ^320

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye paññā pajānanā…pe… amoho dhammavicayo sammādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye amoho hoti. ^321

Ime tasmiṃ samaye tayo hetū honti. ^322

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko phasso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko phasso hoti. ^323

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā vedanā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā vedanā hoti. ^324

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā saññā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā saññā hoti. ^325

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā cetanā hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā sañcetanā cetayitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā cetanā hoti. ^326

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ cittaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ cittaṃ hoti. ^327

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko vedanākkhandho hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ sātaṃ cetasikaṃ sukhaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ sātaṃ sukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā sātā sukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko vedanākkhandho hoti. ^328

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko saññākkhandho hoti? Yā tasmiṃ samaye saññā sañjānanā sañjānitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko saññākkhandho hoti. ^329

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro pīti cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ paññābalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso amoho anabhijjhā abyāpādo sammādiṭṭhi hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajaññaṃ samatho vipassanā paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko saṅkhārakkhandho hoti. ^330

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye eko viññāṇakkhandho hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye eko viññāṇakkhandho hoti. ^331

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ manāyatanaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ manāyatanaṃ hoti. ^332

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ manindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ manindriyaṃ hoti. ^333

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā manoviññāṇadhātu hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ hadayaṃ paṇḍaraṃ mano manāyatanaṃ manindriyaṃ viññāṇaṃ viññāṇakkhandho tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātu – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā manoviññāṇadhātu hoti. ^334

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho , saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti. ^335

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye ekā dhammadhātu hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye ekā dhammadhātu hoti. ⤵ Ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^336

Koṭṭhāsavāro. ^337

<h2>Suññatavāro</h2> ^338

Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye dhammā honti, khandhā honti, āyatanāni honti, dhātuyo honti, āhārā honti, indriyāni honti, jhānaṃ hoti, maggo hoti, balāni honti, hetū honti, phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vedanākkhandho hoti, saññākkhandho hoti, saṅkhārakkhandho hoti, viññāṇakkhandho hoti, manāyatanaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, manoviññāṇadhātu hoti, dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^339

Katame tasmiṃ samaye dhammā honti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ime tasmiṃ samaye dhammā honti. ^340

Katame tasmiṃ samaye khandhā honti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho , saṅkhārakkhandho, viññāṇakkhandho – ime tasmiṃ samaye khandhā honti. ^341

Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye āyatanāni honti? Manāyatanaṃ, dhammāyatanaṃ – imāni tasmiṃ samaye āyatanāni honti. ^342

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye dhātuyo honti? Manoviññāṇadhātu, dhammadhātu – imā tasmiṃ samaye dhātuyo honti. ^343

Katame tasmiṃ samaye āhārā honti? Phassāhāro, manosañcetanāhāro, viññāṇāhāro – ime tasmiṃ samaye āhārā honti. ^344

Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye indriyāni honti? Saddhindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyaṃ, satindriyaṃ, samādhindriyaṃ, paññindriyaṃ, manindriyaṃ, somanassindriyaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ – imāni tasmiṃ samaye indriyāni honti. ^345

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jhānaṃ hoti? Vitakko, vicāro, pīti, sukhaṃ, cittassekaggatā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye jhānaṃ hoti. ^346

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye maggo hoti? Sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappo, sammāvāyāmo, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye maggo hoti. ^347

Katamāni tasmiṃ samaye balāni honti? Saddhābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, satibalaṃ, samādhibalaṃ, paññābalaṃ, hiribalaṃ, ottappabalaṃ – imāni tasmiṃ samaye balāni honti. ^348

Katame tasmiṃ samaye hetū honti? Alobho, adoso, amoho – ime tasmiṃ samaye hetū honti. ^349

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti. ^350

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti. ^351

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññā hoti. ^352

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetanā hoti? ^353

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti…pe… idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cittaṃ hoti. ^354

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandho hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanākkhandho hoti. ^355

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saññākkhandho hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saññākkhandho hoti. ^356

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti. ^357

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandho hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye viññāṇakkhandho hoti. ^358

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manāyatanaṃ hoti…pe… idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manāyatanaṃ hoti. ^359

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti…pe… idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manindriyaṃ hoti. ^360

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye manoviññāṇadhātu hoti…pe… ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye manoviññāṇadhātu hoti. ^361

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammāyatanaṃ hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammāyatanaṃ hoti. ^362

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye dhammadhātu hoti? Vedanākkhandho, saññākkhandho, saṅkhārakkhandho – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye dhammadhātu hoti. ⤵ Ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^363

Suññatavāro. ^364

Paṭhamaṃ cittaṃ. ^365

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^366

Dutiyaṃ cittaṃ. ^367

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, pīti hoti, sukhaṃ hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti , manindriyaṃ hoti, somanassindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammāsaṅkappo hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti, sammāsati hoti, sammāsamādhi hoti, saddhābalaṃ hoti, vīriyabalaṃ hoti, satibalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, hiribalaṃ hoti, ottappabalaṃ hoti, alobho hoti, adoso hoti, anabhijjhā hoti, abyāpādo hoti, hirī hoti, ottappaṃ hoti, kāyapassaddhi hoti, cittapassaddhi hoti, kāyalahutā hoti, cittalahutā hoti, kāyamudutā hoti, cittamudutā hoti, kāyakammaññatā hoti, cittakammaññatā hoti, kāyapāguññatā hoti, cittapāguññatā hoti, kāyujukatā hoti, cittujukatā hoti, sati hoti, samatho hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ⤵ Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, sattindriyāni honti , pañcaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, caturaṅgiko maggo hoti, cha balāni honti, dve hetū honti, eko phasso hoti…pe… ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^368

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro pīti cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammāsaṅkappo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso anabhijjhā abyāpādo hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati samatho paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^369

Tatiyaṃ cittaṃ. ^370

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti somanassasahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^371

Catutthaṃ cittaṃ. ^372

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, upekkhā hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, paññindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, upekkhindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, sammāsaṅkappo hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti, sammāsati hoti, sammāsamādhi hoti, saddhābalaṃ hoti, vīriyabalaṃ hoti, satibalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, paññābalaṃ hoti, hiribalaṃ hoti, ottappabalaṃ hoti, alobho hoti, adoso hoti, amoho hoti, anabhijjhā hoti, abyāpādo hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, hirī hoti, ottappaṃ hoti, kāyapassaddhi hoti, cittapassaddhi hoti, kāyalahutā hoti, cittalahutā hoti, kāyamudutā hoti, cittamudutā hoti, kāyakammaññatā hoti, cittakammaññatā hoti kāyapāguññatā hoti, cittapāguññatā hoti, kāyujukatā hoti, cittujukatā hoti, sati hoti, sampajaññaṃ hoti, samatho hoti, vipassanā hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā. ^373

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti? Yo tasmiṃ samaye phasso phusanā saṃphusanā saṃphusitattaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti. ^374

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye tajjāmanoviññāṇadhātusamphassajaṃ cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye vedanā hoti…pe…. ^375

Katamā tasmiṃ samaye upekkhā hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye upekkhā hoti…pe…. ^376

Katamaṃ tasmiṃ samaye upekkhindriyaṃ hoti? Yaṃ tasmiṃ samaye cetasikaṃ neva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ cetosamphassajaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vedayitaṃ cetosamphassajā adukkhamasukhā vedanā – idaṃ tasmiṃ samaye upekkhindriyaṃ hoti…pe… ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ⤵ Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti , dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, aṭṭhindriyāni honti, caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, pañcaṅgiko maggo hoti, satta balāni honti, tayo hetū honti, eko phasso hoti…pe… ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^377

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ paññābalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso amoho anabhijjhā abyāpādo sammādiṭṭhi hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati sampajaññaṃ samatho vipassanā paggāho avikkhepo. ⤵ Ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^378

Pañcamaṃ cittaṃ. ^379

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇasampayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^380

Chaṭṭhaṃ cittaṃ. ^381

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā gandhārammaṇaṃ vā rasārammaṇaṃ vā phoṭṭhabbārammaṇaṃ vā dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, vitakko hoti, vicāro hoti, upekkhā hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, upekkhindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammāsaṅkappo hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti, sammāsati hoti, sammāsamādhi hoti, saddhābalaṃ hoti, vīriyabalaṃ hoti, satibalaṃ hoti, samādhibalaṃ hoti, hiribalaṃ hoti, ottappabalaṃ hoti, alobho hoti, adoso hoti, anabhijjhā hoti, abyāpādo hoti, hirī hoti, ottappaṃ hoti, kāyapassaddhi hoti, cittapassaddhi hoti, kāyalahutā hoti, cittalahutā hoti, kāyamudutā hoti, cittamudutā hoti, kāyakammaññatā hoti, cittakammaññatā hoti, kāyapāguññatā hoti , cittapāguññatā hoti, kāyujukatā hoti, cittujukatā hoti, sati hoti, samatho hoti, paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ⤵ Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti , sattindriyāni honti, caturaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, caturaṅgiko maggo hoti , cha balāni honti, dve hetū honti, eko phasso hoti…pe… ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^382

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā vitakko vicāro cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammāsaṅkappo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi saddhābalaṃ vīriyabalaṃ satibalaṃ samādhibalaṃ hiribalaṃ ottappabalaṃ alobho adoso anabhijjhā abyāpādo hirī ottappaṃ kāyapassaddhi cittapassaddhi kāyalahutā cittalahutā kāyamudutā cittamudutā kāyakammaññatā cittakammaññatā kāyapāguññatā cittapāguññatā kāyujukatā cittujukatā sati samatho paggāho avikkhepo. ⤵ Ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^383

Sattamaṃ cittaṃ. ^384

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye kāmāvacaraṃ kusalaṃ cittaṃ uppannaṃ hoti upekkhāsahagataṃ ñāṇavippayuttaṃ sasaṅkhārena rūpārammaṇaṃ vā…pe… dhammārammaṇaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ vā panārabbha, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^385

Aṭṭhamaṃ cittaṃ. ^386

Aṭṭha kāmāvacaramahākusalacittāni. ^387

Dutiyabhāṇavāro. ^388

<h1>Rūpāvacarakusalaṃ</h1> ^389

<h2>Catukkanayo</h2> ^390

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṃ savicāraṃ vivekajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ [paṭhamajjhānaṃ (sī.)] upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^391

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ cetaso ekodibhāvaṃ avitakkaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ [dutiyajjhānaṃ (sī.)] upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, pīti hoti, sukhaṃ hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, paññindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, somanassindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti…pe… paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ⤵ Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, aṭṭhindriyāni honti, tivaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, caturaṅgiko maggo hoti, satta balāni honti, tayo hetū honti, eko phasso hoti…pe… ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^392

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā pīti cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāvāyāmo…pe… paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^393

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti pītiyā ca virāgā upekkhako ca viharati sato ca sampajāno sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṃvedeti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – "upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī"ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ [tatiyajjhānaṃ (sī.)] upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti, vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, sukhaṃ hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, paññindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti, somanassindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti…pe… paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ⤵ Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, aṭṭhindriyāni honti, duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, caturaṅgiko maggo hoti, satta balāni honti, tayo hetū honti, eko phasso hoti…pe… ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^394

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāvāyāmo…pe… paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^395

Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva somanassadomanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upekkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ [catutthajjhānaṃ (sī.)] upasampajja viharati pathavīkasiṇaṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti , vedanā hoti, saññā hoti, cetanā hoti, cittaṃ hoti, upekkhā hoti, cittassekaggatā hoti, saddhindriyaṃ hoti, vīriyindriyaṃ hoti, satindriyaṃ hoti, samādhindriyaṃ hoti, paññindriyaṃ hoti, manindriyaṃ hoti upekkhindriyaṃ hoti, jīvitindriyaṃ hoti, sammādiṭṭhi hoti, sammāvāyāmo hoti…pe… paggāho hoti, avikkhepo hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ⤵ Tasmiṃ kho pana samaye cattāro khandhā honti, dvāyatanāni honti, dve dhātuyo honti, tayo āhārā honti, aṭṭhindriyāni honti, duvaṅgikaṃ jhānaṃ hoti, caturaṅgiko maggo hoti, satta balāni honti , tayo hetū honti, eko phasso hoti…pe… ekaṃ dhammāyatanaṃ hoti, ekā dhammadhātu hoti; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā – ime dhammā kusalā…pe…. ^396

Katamo tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti? Phasso cetanā cittassekaggatā saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ paññindriyaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ sammādiṭṭhi sammāvāyāmo…pe… paggāho avikkhepo; ye vā pana tasmiṃ samaye aññepi atthi paṭiccasamuppannā arūpino dhammā ṭhapetvā vedanākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā saññākkhandhaṃ ṭhapetvā viññāṇakkhandhaṃ – ayaṃ tasmiṃ samaye saṅkhārakkhandho hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^397

Pañcakanayo. ^409

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmīti vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ parittaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ…pe… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ appamāṇaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ…pe… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiññaṃ parittaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ…pe… dukkhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiññaṃ appamāṇaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ…pe… sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ parittaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ…pe… sukhapaṭipadaṃ dandhābhiññaṃ appamāṇaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ…pe… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiññaṃ parittaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ…pe… sukhapaṭipadaṃ khippābhiññaṃ appamāṇaṃ appamāṇārammaṇaṃ , tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^500

Aparampi aṭṭhakkhattukaṃ. ^501

<h2>Idampi aṭṭhakkhattukaṃ</h2> ^502

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmīti vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^503

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī bahiddhā rūpāni passati appamāṇāni suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmīti vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ…pe… pañcamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^504

Idampi aṭṭhakkhattukaṃ. ^505

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī bahiddhā rūpāni passati nīlāni nīlavaṇṇāni nīlanidassanāni nīlanibhāsāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmīti vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati , tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^506

Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti ajjhattaṃ arūpasaññī bahiddhā rūpāni passati pītāni pītavaṇṇāni pītanidassanāni pītanibhāsāni…pe… lohitakāni lohitakavaṇṇāni lohitakanidassanāni lohitakanibhāsāni…pe… odātāni odātavaṇṇāni odātanidassanāni odātanibhāsāni, tāni abhibhuyya jānāmi passāmīti vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^507

Imānipi abhibhāyatanāni soḷasakkhattukāni. ^508

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti rūpī rūpāni passati vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^510

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti subhanti vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^512

Imānipi tīṇi vimokkhāni soḷasakkhattukāni. ^513

<h2>Cattāri brahmavihārajhānāni soḷasakkhattukāni</h2> ^514

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vivicceva kāmehi…pe… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati mettāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^515

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti vitakkavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pe… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati mettāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^516

Katame dhammā kusalā? Yasmiṃ samaye rūpūpapattiyā maggaṃ bhāveti pītiyā ca virāgā…pe… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampajja viharati mettāsahagataṃ, tasmiṃ samaye phasso hoti…pe… avikkhepo hoti…pe… ime dhammā kusalā. ^517
